1b1(sna tshogs): @##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya su bi krAn ta bi krA mi pa ri prits+tsha pradz+nyA pA ra mi tA de 
1b2(sna tshogs): sha/bod skad du/'phags pa rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pas zhus pa/shes 
1b3(sna tshogs): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bstan pa/bam po dang po/sangs rgyas dang /byang chub 
1b4(sna tshogs): sems dpa' dang /'phags pa nyan thos thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad 
2a1(sna tshogs): @##//bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab na/'od 
2a2(sna tshogs): ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa na/dge slong stong nyis brgya lnga bcu'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po

2a4(sna tshogs): skye ba gcig gis thogs pa/tshad med grangs med pa dang /thabs gcig tu bzhugs te/de'i 
2a5(sna tshogs): tshe bcom ldan 'das 'khor brgya stong du mas yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas te chos ston to//
2b1(sna tshogs): de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa zhes bya ba 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur nas 
2b2(sna tshogs): 'dug pa de stan las langs te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzar nas/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/bcom 
2b3(sna tshogs): ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//gal te zhus nas zhu ba lung 
2b4(sna tshogs): bstan pa'i slad du/bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la skabs phye na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs 
2b5(sna tshogs): rgyas la phyogs 'ga' zhig zhu lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis/byang chub sems dpa' sems

3a1(sna tshogs): @##//bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la khyod ci dang ci 'dod pa dris shig dang /bris pa de 
3a2(sna tshogs): dang de'i lung bstan pas khyod kyi sems rangs par bya'o//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la/byang chub sems dpa' 
3a3(sna tshogs): sems dpa' chen po rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa shes 
3a4(sna tshogs): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zhes bgyid pa bcom ldan 'das ji tsam gyis na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi shes rab kyi pha rol 
3a5(sna tshogs): tu phyin pa zhes bgyi ba//bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa la spyod pa lags 
3a6(sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa 
3b1(sna tshogs): bsgom pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba lags//bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa na 
3b2(sna tshogs): bdud sdig can gyis glags mi rnyed cing ngang du kyi las thams cad kyang rtogs par 'gyur ba lags/bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' chen po shes
3b3(sna tshogs): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnas pa ji lta bus gnas na myur du thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi chos yongs su rdzogs par 'gyurab lags/de skad ces gsol pa dang /
3b4(sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par
3b5(sna tshogs): gnon pa khyad byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po rnams kyi don dang /ci nas khyod skye bo mang po la phan pa dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'jig rten 
3b6(sna tshogs): la snying brtse ba dang /lha dang mi rnams kyi skye bo phal po che'i don dang /phan pa dang /bde ba'i phyir zhugs shing /da ltar gyi dang ma 'ongs pa'i byang chub sems dpa'
3b7(sna tshogs): sems dpa' chen po rnams la snang ba byed par 'dod pas/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin 
4a1(sna tshogs): @##//ba 'dri ba legs so legs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis mkhyen bzhin du/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zas kyi rtsal gyis rgyas par gnon 
4a2(sna tshogs): pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa khyod don gyi bar gang mthong nas de bzhin gshegs pa la don 'di lta bu 'di/de skad ces 
4a3(sna tshogs): bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems1 dpa' chen po rab kyi rtsal gyis rgyas par bzhon pas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 
4a4(sna tshogs): 'das sems can thams cad la sman pa dang /sems can thams cad la snying brtse ba dang /sems can thams chad kyi don gyi slad du bdag de bzhin gshegs pa la de na 
4a5(sna tshogs): 'di zhu lags so//de ci'i slad du de na/bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /byang chub 
4a6(sna tshogs): sems dpa' dang /yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos dang /chos thams cad sdud pa lags pa'i slad du'o//bcom ldan 'das de la sems can gang dag 
4a7(sna tshogs): nyan thos kyi theg pa la nges par gyur pa de dag gis thos na myur du zag pa ma mchis pa'i sa mngon sum du bgyid par 'gyur ba dang /gang dag rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa 
4b1(sna tshogs): la nges par gyur ba de dag kyang myur du rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pas nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ba dang /gang dag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la yang dag par zhugs pa de dag kyang myur du bla na med pa 
4b2(sna tshogs): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byed chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ba dang /gang? dag yang dag pa nyid skyon med pa la ma zhugs shing sa gsum lam nges pa de dag gis thos na bla na med pa yang dag 
4b3(sna tshogs): par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed par 'gyur ba dang /bcom lnga 'das de bzhin gshegs pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di zhus pa'i lan btab pas/sems can thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba 
4b4(sna tshogs): bskyed par 'gyur ba'i slad du/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes bstan du gsol/bcom ldan 'das bdag ni sems can dman pa la mos pa rnams kyi slad du de 
4b5(sna tshogs): bzhin gshegs pa la zhu ba ma lags/dbul ba'i sems dang ldan pa rnams kyi ma lags/dbul po'i theg pa la yang dag par zhugs pa rnams kyi slad du ma lags/le lo can rnams 
4b6(sna tshogs): kyi slad du ma lags/le lo'i zil gyis non pa rnams kyi slad du ma la gas/lta ba'i 'dam du bying ba rnams kyi slad du ma lags /bdud kyi zhags pas zin pa rnams kyi slad du mal gas/
4b7(sna tshogs): ngo tsha mang tshes cing khrel ma mchis pa rnams kyi slad du malags/yo byad bsnyungs pa dang mi ldan pa rnams kyi slad du ma legs/bskyed ngas pa rnams kyi slad du ma? lags/
5a1(sna tshogs): @#//sems 'khrul pa rnams kyi slad ma lags/'dod pa'i 'dam du bying ba rnams kyi slad ma lags/
5a2(sna tshogs): slad ma lags/brtsis pa mi 'jo ba rnams kyi slad du ma lags/sdig pa 'tshol ba rnams kyi slad du ma la gas/sdig pa spyod pa rnams kyi slad ma lags /rgyu can rnams kyi 
5a3(sna tshogs): khrims nyams pa rnams kyi slad ma lags/tshul khrims yongs su ma dag pa rnams kyi slad ma lags
5a4(sna tshogs): /lta ba ma nyams pa rnams kyi slad du ma lags /bdud kyi 
5a5(sna tshogs): spyod yul la spyod pa rnams kyi slad du ma lan pa/bdag la bstod pa rnams kyi slad ma laTas/gzhan la smod pa rnams kyi slad du ma 'gas/rnyed pa dang bkur sti la 
5a6(sna tshogs): gdu ba rnams kyi slad ma lagsthung bzed dang chos gos la chags pa rnams kyi slad ma la gas/tshul 'chos pa rnams kyi slad du ma legs/kha gsag cing rnams 
5a7(sna tshogs): kyi slad ma lags/gzhog slongs can rnams kyi slad ma lags/thob kyis 'jal ba rnams kyi slad ma lags rnyed pa la rnyed par sgrub pa rnams kyi slad ma lags/
5a8(sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das sems can de lta bu rnams kyi slad 'dag de bzhin gshegs pa la zhu ba ma lagaso//bcom ldan 'das sems cad gzhan dag thams cad mkhyen pa'i yi 
5b1(sna tshogs): shes tshol ba dang /chags pa ma mchis pa'i ye shes dang /rang byung gi ye shes dang /mi mnyam pa'i ye shes dang /bla na med pa'i ye shes tshol ba de dag dang /gang dag bdag 
5b2(sna tshogs): dang pha rol kyang mi dmigs na/bdag la stod pa dang /gzhan la smod pa lta ga la mchis te/bcom ldan 'das ra bcad pa lta bur nga rgyal ma mchis pa/zug rngu phyung ba 
5b3(sna tshogs): gdog pa lta bur sems dma' ba/sems sa dang /chu dang /me dang /rlung dang /nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa'i sems can rnams dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems 
5b4(sna tshogs): dpa' chen po rnams kyi slad du bdag de bzhin gshegs pa la zhu lags so//bcom ldan 'das gang dag chos kyang mi dmigs shing rlom sems su mi bgyid la mngon par zhen pa ma mchis 
5b5(sna tshogs): na|chos ma lags pa lta ga la mchis te/byang chub sems dpa' ses dpa' chen po de dag gi slad bdag de bzhin gshegs ba la zhu ba lags so//bcom ldan 'dis bsam 
5b6(sna tshogs): pa dag pa/g.yo ma mchis pa/sgyu ma mchis pa/drang ba/sems mnyam pa/sems can thams cad la sbyan pa dang /snying brtse ba can yang dag par 'dzin du stsol ba/
5b7(sna tshogs): yang dag par gzeng bstod pa/yang dag par dga' bar bgyid pa/khur chen po khyer ba/theg pa chen po la mngon par zhugs pa/bgyi ba chen pos so sor nye bar gnas pa/
6a1(sna tshogs): @#//snying rje chen po dang ldan pa/sems can thams cad bde ba thob par bgyid pa/'dren pa/rnam par 'dren pa/yongs su 'dren pa/chos thams cad la mi brten 
6a2(sna tshogs): par gnas pa/skye ba'i gnas thams cad mi tshol ba/'dud kyi zhags pa thams cad las rnam par grol ba/'dun pa can brtson 'grus dang ldan pa/bag mchis pa/chos 
6a3(sna tshogs): thams cad kyi dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa/the tshom thams cad gcod pa la mkhas pa'i sems can rnams dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi slad du 
6a4(sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das bdag de bzhin gshegs pa la zhu lags so//bcom ldan 'das sems can gang dag sangs rgyas kyi ye shes la yang rlom sems su mi bgyid cing mngon par mi 
6a5(sna tshogs): zhen la chags par mi gnas shing /rlom sems thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa'i lam la gnas pa/lam du zhugs pa/lam ston pa'i sems can rnams 
6a6(sna tshogs): dang|byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po de dag gi slad du/bcom ldan 'das bdag de de bzhin gshegs pa la zha lugs so//bcom ldan 'das/bdag ni sems 
6a7(sna tshogs): can thams cad la bde ba dang /lhag na ma mchis pa'i bde ba dang /bla na ma mchis pa'i bde ba ba dang /mya ngan las 'das pa'i bde ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi bde ba dang /
6b1(sna tshogs): 'dus ma 'byis pa'i bde ba nye bar bsgrub par 'tshal te/bdag sems can thams cad kyi don dang /sman pa dang /bde ba dang /grub pa dang /bde ba'i slad du de bzhin gshegs pa las su 
6b2(sna tshogs): lag ngo//bcom ldan 'das bdag ni the tshom ma mchis par 'tshal zhing /the tshom mchis nas kyang sems can rnams kyi the tshom rab tu spong ba'i slad du chos bshad par 'tshal te/
6b3(sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das bdag sems can thams can kyi the tshom bsal ba'i slad du bcom ldan 'das la zhu lags so//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das sems can thams cad ni sdug bsngal 
6b4(sna tshogs): dang mi mthun zhing bde ba 'tshal la/sems can thams cad ni bde ba don du gnyer ba lags te/bcom ldan 'das shes rab ma gtogs par gzhan sems can rnams kyi bde ba 
6b5(sna tshogs): gang yang bdag gis ma mthong zhing /bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa/theg pa chen po ma gtogs par sems can rnams kyi bde pa gang yang ma mchis te/bcom 
6b6(sna tshogs): ldan 'das bdag gis don gyi dbang 'di mthong bas sems can rnams la bde ba nye bar bsgrubs pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zhu lags so//bcom ldan 'das byang chub 
6b7(sna tshogs): sems dpa' rnams kyi don 'di yang dag par mthong bas bdag gi de bzhin gshegs pa la don 'di zhu lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems 
7a1(sna tshogs): @#//dpar sems dpa' chen po rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//zab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par bzhon pa legs so legs so//khyod skye bo 
7a2(sna tshogs): phal po che la snying brtse ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dri ba ci khyod kyi yon tan gyi mtha' rtogs par sla ba ma yin no//de'i phyir rab kyi rtsal gyis 
7a3(sna tshogs): rnam par gnon pa legs par nyon la yid la rab tu zung shig dang /ngas khyod la bshad do//bcom ldan 'das legs so zhes gsol nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
7a4(sna tshogs): chen po rab kyi rtsal gyis rnal par gnon pa bcom ldan 'das kyi lhar nyan pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal te//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gon pa 
7a5(sna tshogs): khyod 'di skad du/bcom ldan 'das khyod shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa zhes bgyi ba bcom ldan 'das ji tsam gyis na/byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
7a6(sna tshogs): kyi shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa zhes bgyi zhes zer ba ni rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa ni chos gang gis kyang brjod par bya ba ma yin te/shes 
7a7(sna tshogs): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni tshig thams cad las 'das pa'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnams par gnon pa shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa ni/'di shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//
7b1(sna tshogs): 'di'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//de'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa'o zhes brjod phar mi nus so//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa 'di ni chos thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin 
7b2(sna tshogs): pa yin te/de'i phyir shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa zhes bya'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnams par gnon pa/shes rab nyid kyang de bzhin gshegs pas ma rnyed ma dmigs na shes rab 
7b3(sna tshogs): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa lta dmigs par ga la 'gyur/rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par bzhon pa shes rab ces bya ba 'di ni chos thams cad kun du shes pa 'di ni chos thams can kun tu rig pa 
7b4(sna tshogs): ste de'i phyir shes rab ces bya'o//rab kyi rtsal gyi rnam par gnon pa/chos thams cad kun tu rig pa gang zhes na/chos 'di dag thams cad ni rnam pa gzhan na chos thams cad 
7b5(sna tshogs): rnam pa gzhan du brjod de/chos thams cad ni brjod pa las rnam par grol ba ma yin no//gang chos thams cad kun tu shes pa dang /gang chos thams cad kun du rig pa de yang 
7b6(sna tshogs): tshig gis brjod par mi nus mod gyi/'on kyang ji ltar sems can rnams kyis shes par 'gyur ba de ltar shes rab ces bya'o//'di ni btags pa zhes bya ste/de'i phyir shes rab ces 
7b7(sna tshogs): bya'o||rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa/chos rnams cad ni gang gsu med/gzung du med/bshad du med/snang ba med/de lta bur kun du rig pa de ni kun du rig pa zhes 
8a1(sna tshogs): @#//bya'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa shes rab ces bya ba de ni shes pa'ang ma yin kun tu mi shes pa'ang ma yin de las kyang ma yin de las ma yin pa yang ma 
8a2(sna tshogs): yin te de'i phyir shes ba ces bya'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rna ma par gnon pa de ni mi shes pa'i spyod yul yang ma yin/ye shes kyi spyod yul yang ma yin/mi shes pa'i yul yang 
8a3(sna tshogs): ma yin|ye shes kyi yul yang ma yin te/ye shes ni yul med pa'o//gal te ye shes la yul yod du zin na mi shes par 'gyur ro//ye shes ni mi shes pa las ma 
8a4(sna tshogs): yin|mi shes pa'ang ye shes las ma yin/ye shes ni mi shes pa ma yin/ye shes pa yang ye shes ma yin te/ye shes kyis ye shes shes bya'o//ye shes 
8a5(sna tshogs): kyis mi shes pa zhes bya'o//mi shes pas ye shes shes bya'o//de la ye shes de ni 'di'o//ye shes de ni 'di'o//ye zhes de ni 'di'o//ye shes da ni 'di'i phyir ro zhes 
8a6(sna tshogs): ye shes cung zad kyang bstan par mi nus te/de'i phyir ye shes don ye shes nyid kyis med do//ye shes kyang ye shes su mi gnas te/mi shes pas kyang ye shes shes ma bya'o//
8a7(sna tshogs): gal te mi shes pas ye shes shes pa na ni/de'i phyir byis pa so so'i skye bo thams cad kyang ye shes can du 'gyur ro//gang ye shes dang mi shes pa mi dmigs shing ye shes 
8b1(sna tshogs): dang mi shes pa yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs su shes pa de nyid ye shes shes bya ste/ye shes ni ji skad du brjod pa de bzhin du ma yin no//de bas na ye shes ni brjod par bya ba 
8b2(sna tshogs): ma yin zhing /yul thams cad las rab tu 'das pa ste/ye shes ni gang gi yul yang ma yin no//ye shes kyi yul yang gang yang ma yin no//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par 
8b3(sna tshogs): gnon pa/ye shes bstan pa'i yul yang ma yin/phyogs kyang ma yin pa 'di gang gis shes pa de ni ye shes can nam ye shes can ma yin pa zhes bya ba'i grangs su mi 'gro'o//
8b4(sna tshogs): rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa/gang 'di ltar rab tu shes pa dang rjes su rtogs pa dang kun tu shes pa de ni shes rab ces bya'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam bar gnon pa/gang 
8b5(sna tshogs): 'di ltar mngon par rtogs pa mngon sum du byed pa de ni 'jig rten las 'das pa'i shes rab ces bya'o//'jig rten las 'das pa'i shas rab ces ji skad du brjod pa bzhin du ni ma 
8b6(sna tshogs): yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jig rten nyid kyang mi dmigs na 'jig rten las 'das ba'i shes rab lta ga la yod/'jig rten las 'das pa'i shis rab kyis gang 'jig rten las sgrol 
8b7(sna tshogs): bar 'gyur ba lta ci smos/de ci'i phyir zhe na/des 'jig rten mi dmigs pas ci yang sgrol bar mi byed de/de'i phyir 'jig rten las 'das pa shes rab ces bya'o//rabs kyi rtsal 
9a1(sna tshogs): @#//gyis rnam par gnon pa 'jig rten zhes bya ba 'di ni btags pa zhes brjod pa ste/btags pa ni 'jig rten las yang dag par 'das pa ma yin gyi/btags pa thams cad 
9a2(sna tshogs): las yang dag par 'das ba ni 'jig rten las 'das pa zhes bya'o//'da' bar bya ba ni 'jig rten las 'dus pa ma yin gyi 'da' bar mi bya ba ni 'jig rten las bdas pa'o//de ci'i 
9a3(sna tshogs): phyir zhe na/de la gang 'da' bar bya ba dang /gang gis 'da' bar bya ba'i chos phra rab tsam yang med pas de'i phyir 'das pa zhes bya ste/'jig rten las 'das pa la ni 'jig rten 
9a4(sna tshogs): yang med/'jig rten las 'das ba yang med do//bla na med pa la ni bsgral ba med/de'i phyir 'jig rten las 'das par zhes bya'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa/
9a5(sna tshogs): 'di ni 'jig rten las 'das ba'i shes rab bstan pa zhes bya ste/'jig rtena'ajig rten las 'das pa'i shes rab ces ji skad du brjod pa bzhin du ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/
9a6(sna tshogs): 'jig rten las 'das pa ni brjod par bya ba ma yin gyi de ni brgal ba ste/de la ni 'da' bar bya ba ci yang med pas de'i phyir jig rten las 'das pa'i she rab ces bya'o//
9a7(sna tshogs): rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par bzhon pa de la gang nges par rtogs pa'i shes rab des ci nges par rtogs/'di la ni nges par rtogs par bya ba ci yang med do//gal te nges par rtogs 
9b1(sna tshogs): par bya ba 'ga' zhig yod du zin na ni 'di shes rab kyis nges par rtogs pa zhes gdags na gang gis kyang mi rtogs shing /kun rtogs pa ma yin la/gang gis rtogs par 'gyur 
9b2(sna tshogs): ba'i bye phrag gang yang mi dmigs so//nges par rtogs pa zhes bya ba ni 'di la rtogs par byed pa ci yang med cing kun tu rtogs par bya ba yang med/de la rtogs par byed pa ci yong med 
9b3(sna tshogs): cing kun tu rtogs par bya pa yang med pas de'i phyir nges par rtogs pa zhes bya'o//'di la ni gang yang mthar 'gro ba med cing dpung du yang ma yin te/de'i phyir nges par rtogs ba zhes 
9b4(sna tshogs): bya'o||nges par rtogs pa'i shes rab kyis rtogs pa ni steng ma rtogs te/gang du yang mi rgyug rnam par mi rgyug la/kun tu mi rgyug ste/de'i phyir nges par rtog pa zhes bya'o|/
9b5(sna tshogs): rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon pa/nges par rtogs shes bya ba'i shes rab kyis ci nges par rtogs she na/gang ci snang ba de thams cad nges par rtogs so//gang gis nges par 
9b6(sna tshogs): rtogs she na/shes rab kyis//shes rab kyis ji ltar nges par rtogs she na//gang brtags pa'i mtshan nyid thams cad mtshan nyid med pa dang mtshan nyid med pa yang btags pa'i 
9b7(sna tshogs): mtshan nyid du'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par bzhon pa/gang 'di lta bu'i shes rab dang ldan pas nges par rtogs pa des ni khams gsum rtogs so//ji ltar rtogs she na/
10a1(sna tshogs): @#//khams gsum ni khams med par rtogs te/'di la ni khams rtogs pa ci yang med do//des khams gsum ni khams med bar rtogs so//gang de ltar khams

10a3(sna tshogs): par rtogs pa'i shes rab ba dang ldan zhe na nges par rtogs par bya ba ni mi dge ba gang yang med do/des thams cad dge bar nges par rtogs te/dngos par rtogs pa'i shes

10b6(sna tshogs): ba dang ldan pas chags pa med pa ni gsum rig pa zhes bya'o//rab kyi rtsal gyis rnams par gnon pa/rig pa zhes bya ba de ni ma rig pa nye bar zhi ba'i tshig bla dgas so//

11a1(sna tshogs): pa mkhas pa/gsal ba/yid rno ba/lhag par rtogs pa dang ldan pa/sman thams cad la mkhas pa/nad ldang ba thams cad la mkhas pa/sdug bsngal

11b1(sna tshogs): de ni 'byung ba dang nub pa rab tu rtogs pa'i tshig bla dgas so//'byung ba dang nub pa gang zhe na/kun 'byung ba'i chos ci yang rung ste/de thams cad ni 'gog pa'i chos te/'di ltar

16a1(sna tshogs): @#//

18a1(sna tshogs): @#//can thams cad ni yod pa ma yin pa la dmigs pas sems can dang /sems can thams cad ni rang gi spyod pa rnam par ma rung bar byas pas sems can dang /sems
